**NVA-97 / LEGACY INTERFACE — ACTIVE SESSION**

Angel freezes the log.

**REN**

> Nova?

**ANGEL**

> That's what the identifier says.

**REN**

> But Nova was destroyed.

A technician approaches.

**TECHNICIAN**

> Legacy compatibility module. Completely harmless.

Angel turns.

**ANGEL**

> Is it?

**TECHNICIAN**

> It's old code. Nothing more.

The technician resets the display.

Angel glances at the independent diagnostic buffer on a small handheld instrument.

The activity was recorded before the reset.

**ANGEL**

> Old code shouldn't need a new session.

The technician has already walked away.

Ren looks thoughtful.

CUT TO BLACK.

---

# SCENE 5 — LET THE FIGHTING BEGIN

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 1]**

**INT. MAIN ARENA**

Tens of thousands of spectators fill the stadium.

The arena floor consists of reinforced interlocking platforms surrounded by protective barriers.

Gigantic screens display the competitors.

Shirro appears above the main platform.

**SHIRRO**

> LADIES AND GENTLEMEN! I have been waiting TEN YEARS to say this!

The crowd responds.

**SHIRRO**

> LET THE FIGHTING BEGIN!

A wave of lights sweeps across the arena.

**ALEX (BROADCAST)**

> Twelve pilots, four qualifying groups, and only eight places in the knockout stage.

**LEENA (BROADCAST)**

> Today's opening match features two competitors with very different reasons for wanting this championship.

The screens show Ari and Selene.

**ALEX**

> Ari Solano. Ganymede-born, independent, and already making some powerful people uncomfortable.

**LEENA**

> And Selene Vale—an ambitious private entrant whose preparations have been almost as difficult to investigate as her business connections.

**ALEX**

> That sounds like a compliment.

**LEENA**

> It wasn't intended as one.

The camera follows Ari entering the preparation platform.

For the default presentation, Ari's HAR is a rugged, customized Thorn.

Selene's default HAR is the sleek Mirage.

**[TECHNICAL]** Dialogue must remain valid when the player selects different HAR models. Signature HAR imagery is substituted by the actual selection.

## SCENE 5A — PRE-FIGHT

**SELENE**

> Ari Solano. I expected someone taller.

**ARI**

> I expected someone less interested in appearances.

**SELENE**

> Appearances are rather important in this business. Particularly when you're trying to become a symbol.

**ARI**

> I'm here to win.

**SELENE**

> Of course. The question is what you'll do when you discover how useful winning can be.

Ari's HAR moves into position.

**ARI**

> Maybe you should worry about losing.

Selene smiles.

**SELENE**

> I never enter a negotiation without considering the possibility.

### [FIGHT 1 — ARI VS. SELENE]

**Gameplay:** Normal HAR combat.

Selene's default Mirage can demonstrate stylish feints and deceptive positioning.

Ari's default Thorn emphasizes robust counters and pressure.

No narrative interference occurs during this match.

**Default outcome:** Ari wins.

## SCENE 5B — POST-FIGHT

Selene emerges from her damaged HAR platform.

Ari approaches.

**SELENE**

> Congratulations. That was irritatingly effective.

**ARI**

> You're taking defeat well.

**SELENE**

> Am I? I'll have to work on that.

She looks toward the cheering crowd.

**SELENE**

> You have their attention now.

**ARI**

> Good.

**SELENE**

> Be careful what you do with it. Attention attracts opportunities.

**ARI**

> And obligations?

Selene smiles.

**SELENE**

> You're learning already.

She walks away.

**ALEX (BROADCAST)**

> A decisive opening victory for Solano!

**LEENA**

> Selene Vale may have underestimated the newcomer.

**ALEX**

> You think she'll admit it?

**LEENA**

> Not publicly.

The camera finds Crystal watching from a private competitors' area.

She seems interested in Ari's performance.

---

# SCENE 6 — A BETTER KIND OF ENTERTAINMENT

**[SHORT CINEMATIC — DAY 2]**

**INT. ARENA / BROADCAST MONTAGE**

Mara faces Raven in another qualifying match.

Her patched Flail looks rough compared with Raven's polished Katana.

**ALEX**

> Mara Voss against Raven! Experience versus experience, though perhaps from rather different schools.

**LEENA**

> Both fighters are dangerous at close range. I wouldn't recommend standing between them.

A rapid montage shows Mara absorbing pressure, finding an opening, and delivering a decisive strike.

Raven's HAR collapses.

The crowd cheers.

**ALEX**

> VOSS WINS!

After the match, Mara approaches Raven.

**RAVEN**

> You're better than I expected.

**MARA**

> You expected me to be bad?

**RAVEN**

> I expected you to be reckless.

**MARA**

> You should've watched more closely.

Raven almost smiles.

**RAVEN**

> I will next time.

CUT TO:

---

# SCENE 7 — A LESSON IN BAD HABITS

**[CINEMATIC — FIRST TRAINING SCENE]**

**INT. COMPETITORS' PRACTICE FACILITY — LATE AFTERNOON**

The training room is quiet.

Two practice simulators stand opposite each other.

Mara is adjusting a mechanical joint on her equipment when Ari enters.

**ARI**

> I heard you beat Raven.

**MARA**

> News travels fast.

**ARI**

> Impressive performance.

**MARA**

> Thank you. I was hoping he'd make it more difficult.

**ARI**

> Really?

**MARA**

> No. I'm not an idiot.

Ari laughs.

Mara wipes her hands.

**MARA**

> I saw your match too.

**ARI**

> And?

**MARA**

> You telegraph your right-side counter.

**ARI**

> I won.

**MARA**

> You did. Congratulations on defeating someone who didn't notice.

**ARI**

> You offering to help?

**MARA**

> That depends. Are you offering to learn?

Ari gestures toward the practice simulators.

**ARI**

> Show me.

They activate a low-power training session.

**[OPTIONAL INTERACTIVE DRILL]**

Mara demonstrates a feint that punishes Ari's habitual defensive response.

Ari initially falls for it.

**MARA**

> Again.

Ari adapts.

Mara counters the adaptation.

**ARI**

> You enjoy this.

**MARA**

> Immensely.

They try again.

Ari successfully catches Mara's opening.

**ARI**

> There.

**MARA**

> Better.

**ARI**

> Only better?

**MARA**

> Don't get greedy.

The simulators shut down.

They step away from the controls.

For the first time, the conversation grows slightly quieter.

**ARI**

> Thanks.

**MARA**

> Don't thank me yet. Now I know what you'll do when we fight.

**ARI**

> You're assuming we'll fight.

**MARA**

> It's a fighting tournament, Ari.

**ARI**

> Fair point.

Her wrist communicator vibrates.

A private call appears.

**INCOMING: MOM**

Mara sees it and her expression changes.

She silences the notification.

Ari notices but doesn't pry.

**ARI**

> Everything all right?

**MARA**

> Yeah. Just something at home.

She forces a small smile.

**MARA**

> You have a surprisingly patient face for somebody who talks to corporate executives like they're malfunctioning appliances.

**ARI**

> Is that a compliment?

**MARA**

> I haven't decided.

A beat.

**ARI**

> I liked talking to you yesterday.

Mara looks at Ari.

**MARA**

> I noticed.

**ARI**

> Was it that obvious?

**MARA**

> Painfully.

She picks up her bag.

**MARA**

> Same time tomorrow?

**ARI**

> Another lesson?

**MARA**

> Maybe I'll even teach you something useful.

**ARI**

> You already did.

Mara smiles, more sincerely this time.

**MARA**

> Careful, Solano. Keep talking like that and I'll start charging you.

She leaves.

Ari remains in the practice room, looking pleased and a little surprised.

---

# SCENE 8 — PRIVATE OBSERVATIONS

**[SHORT CINEMATIC — CHAPTER STINGER]**

**INT. RESEARCH DIVISION — NIGHT**

Varga sits alone in her office.

The public broadcast displays excerpts from the opening tournament matches.

On a private screen, she examines neural synchronization measurements.

She switches to an unauthorized file.

**AFFECTIVE RESPONSE STUDY — PRIVATE**

Several entries appear.

**STEFFAN / DANTE — COMPETITIVE ANTAGONISM**

**LYRA / STEFFAN — SOCIAL ATTENTION**

**ARI / MARA — INITIAL AFFILIATION**

Varga reviews a recording of the gala.

Ari laughs at something Mara has said.

She examines the time stamp and adds a note.

**VARGA**

> Interesting.

A notification appears.

**NII CENTRAL SYSTEM: EXTERNAL DIAGNOSTIC SESSION COMPLETE**

Varga dismisses it without opening the message.

The camera moves toward a second, unattended monitor.

A process associated with the obsolete NVA-97 interface has requested synchronization statistics from the tournament network.

The request is silently approved by the central research system.

CUT TO:

**INT. RESTRICTED LABORATORY**

A sealed chamber.

A life-support indicator pulses.

A neural waveform appears on a dark display.

The recording shows no face or identifying information.

A quiet synthetic tone confirms an active connection.

FADE OUT.

**END OF CHAPTER 1**

---

# ARCADE MODE — COMPRESSED VERSION

The arcade introduction should preserve the mood and basic stakes without requiring the full gala.

## Arcade opening cinematic

**[Approximately 35–45 seconds]**

Quick cuts of Crystal's 2097 victory, the anniversary arena, the returning legends, and the six newcomers.

**SHIRRO:** Ten years ago, Crystal Devroe defeated Nova. Tonight, twelve fighters compete for a new championship!

**ALEX:** New technology, new rivalries, and a Ganymede defense contract worth billions.

Ari looks toward the arena.

**MARA:** You look like you're planning a revolution.

**ARI:** I'm planning to win.

**MARA:** Try smiling first. It'll confuse them.

Steffan smiles at Lyra.

Dante scowls.

Jean-Paul exchanges a look with Varga.

Angel notices a Nova-associated diagnostic symbol.

CUT TO FIGHT.

## Fight 1 — Ari vs. Selene

**SELENE:** You want to challenge WAR? Winning here might be the most dangerous way to begin.

**ARI:** Then I'd better make it count.

**[FIGHT]**

After victory:

**SELENE:** Well fought. Now let's see what you do with the attention.

Mara watches the result on a broadcast screen.

**MARA:** Not bad, troublemaker.

## Arcade transition

A brief montage shows Mara defeating Raven.

Angel examines the obsolete Nova identifier.

**ANGEL:** That system shouldn't exist.

A black screen displays:

**NVA-97 — CONNECTION ACTIVE**

The arcade route continues to Ari's second qualifying match against Crystal.

---

# IMPLEMENTATION NOTES

## Narrative flags

**MARA_RAPPORT_1**

- Choice A: Shared humor.
- Choice B: Mutual curiosity.
- Choice C: Early flirtation.

These adjust later dialogue but do not lock or unlock the romance.

**SELENE_FIRST_MATCH**

Tracks match outcome and whether the player has already encountered her in later story branches.

**ANGEL_NOVA_CLUE_1**

Established automatically in the shared timeline.

The user does not need to select an investigative dialogue option to preserve the main mystery.

## Technical continuity

- Selene's corporate background remains partially concealed.
- Kreissack's survival is not disclosed.
- The NVA-97 identifier only establishes that old technology is currently active.
- Varga's unauthorized experiment is foreshadowed but not explicitly explained.
- Nova's access to synchronization statistics does not mean it can directly integrate or control all pilots.
- No current pilot is secretly fully assimilated in this chapter.
- Ari and Mara's attraction arises independently of Varga.
- Lyra is a recognized elite athlete and celebrity from her first appearance.

## Chapter design targets

**Cinematic Mode:** Approximately 6–9 minutes of mandatory scenes, with optional character vignettes and training interaction, plus a full playable match.

**Arcade Mode:** Approximately 1–2 minutes of total story presentation around the match.

These are initial pacing targets, subject to actual voice and animation timing.

## Intended player impressions

By the end of the chapter, the player should feel that:

1. The anniversary championship is an exciting event.
2. The returning characters have histories worth discovering.
3. Ari has a serious but not yet fully explained grievance against WAR.
4. Mara is entertaining, competent, and potentially important to Ari.
5. Steffan, Lyra, and Dante form a volatile relationship triangle.
6. Jean-Paul and Varga have an intriguing attraction.
7. Something associated with Nova may have survived.

The player should **not** yet suspect that Nova has developed an independent composite intelligence or that Kreissack is still alive.

---

# ONE MUST FALL 2107
## Chapter 2 — The Champion's Shadow

**Screenplay:** v0.1  
**Timeline:** Tournament Days 3–4  
**Primary playable fight:** Ari vs. Crystal  
**Main characters:** Ari, Crystal, Mara, Lyra, Steffan, Dante  
**Secondary appearances:** Jean-Paul, Varga, Raven, Ren, Angel, Milano, Shirro

### Chapter objectives

- Establish Lyra's independence, athletic skill, and celebrity appeal.
- Develop the Steffan–Lyra attraction while retaining Dante's dignity and competitiveness.
- Advance Ari and Mara from playful acquaintances toward personal trust.
- Confront Ari with Crystal's imperfect but sincere legacy.
- Introduce further hints of neural-interface irregularities.
- Demonstrate that player victories genuinely change tournament outcomes.

---

# SCENE 1 — ELECTRIC CELEBRITY

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 3, AFTERNOON]**

**INT. CHAMPIONSHIP ARENA**

A deafening crowd chants Lyra's name.

Holographic projections display Electra's silhouette above thousands of spectators.

Lyra's modeling campaigns, sponsorships, and athletic achievements appear across the broadcast montage.

**ALEX (BROADCAST)**

And here comes the face of a dozen international campaigns—and the pilot who made last year's exhibition circuit look easy!

**LEENA (BROADCAST)**

Three major championships, Alex. She didn't get here by being photogenic.

**ALEX**

Though I imagine it hasn't hurt.

**LEENA**

Neither has being extremely good at fighting.

Lyra's Electra faces Milano's Gargoyle.

A short montage shows an intense exchange. Milano uses heavy defensive positioning, but Lyra anticipates his counterattack.

Electra lands a precise finishing sequence.

**SHIRRO**

LYRA KISARAGI TAKES THE MATCH!

The crowd erupts.

Lyra raises both hands, victorious.

A sea of illuminated Electra symbols ripples through the stadium.

She spots a group of young fans near the front row and points toward them.

They scream with excitement.

**[CUT TO BACKSTAGE]**

Lyra is drying her hair when Steffan appears beside the dressing-room entrance.

**STEFFAN**

That final counter. Beautiful.

**LYRA**

You actually watched the fight?

**STEFFAN**

Every second.

**LYRA**

That's unusual. Most people just watch the entrance.

**STEFFAN**

The entrance was excellent too.

Lyra smiles despite herself.

**LYRA**

Careful. I might think you're trying to flatter me.

**STEFFAN**

I wouldn't insult your intelligence.

**LYRA**

That's encouraging.

**STEFFAN**

I'd rather challenge your confidence.

**LYRA**

You'd need an appointment. My confidence has a very busy schedule.

Steffan laughs.

**STEFFAN**

You know, when you and I meet, I intend to win.

**LYRA**

That's adorable.

**STEFFAN**

Adorable?

**LYRA**

Most men at least pretend to be intimidated.

**STEFFAN**

Maybe I should buy you dinner and practice.

Lyra considers the invitation.

**LYRA**

I'm having dinner with Dante.

Steffan's expression barely changes.

**STEFFAN**

Of course.

**LYRA**

And I'm not sure you'd survive the conversation.

**STEFFAN**

I'll take that as an invitation to improve my conversational skills.

She turns toward her fans.

**LYRA**

Then start by learning when someone has to leave.

She walks away, smiling.

Steffan watches her go.

Dante steps out from behind a nearby equipment display.

**DANTE**

Enjoying yourself?

**STEFFAN**

Enormously.

**DANTE**

Stay away from her.

**STEFFAN**

Why? Afraid she might decide I'm more entertaining?

**DANTE**

Afraid you'll make an idiot of yourself in public.

Steffan steps closer.

**STEFFAN**

That concern almost sounds affectionate.

**DANTE**

Don't flatter yourself.

**STEFFAN**

You make that difficult.

Dante stares at him.

**DANTE**

You'll get your chance against me.

**STEFFAN**

Good.

A pause.

**STEFFAN**

I'd hate to think you were avoiding me.

Steffan walks away.

Dante remains tense, then notices a broadcast replay of Lyra's victory.

His expression softens into genuine pride.

---

# SCENE 2 — BREAKFAST WITH THE ENEMY

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 3, MORNING]**

**INT. COMPETITORS' CAFETERIA**

Ari studies the tournament schedule on a handheld screen.

Mara places a breakfast tray opposite Ari without asking permission.

**MARA**

You're going to get wrinkles.

**ARI**

What?

**MARA**

That expression. You've been making it for three consecutive minutes.

**ARI**

I'm reviewing the match schedule.

**MARA**

That's what the expression says.

Ari looks up.

**ARI**

Good morning to you too.

**MARA**

See? Better already.

She takes a seat.

The screen shows the remaining Group A match:

**ARI SOLANO vs. CRYSTAL DEVROE**

**MARA**

Ah. The living legend.

**ARI**

You sound impressed.

**MARA**

I am impressed. She's very good.

**ARI**

I know.

**MARA**

That doesn't sound like you're impressed.

**ARI**

She won the championship ten years ago. WAR still uses her victory to advertise itself.

**MARA**

And she doesn't work for them anymore.

**ARI**

That doesn't mean she has nothing to answer for.

Mara considers this.

**MARA**

Maybe not. But you're fighting the woman, not the advertising department.

Ari pauses.

**ARI**

You think I'm being unfair?

**MARA**

I think you sometimes enter an argument before anyone has started one.

Ari smiles reluctantly.

**ARI**

You're unusually perceptive for someone who steals pastries.

**MARA**

The pastries are how I maintain my powers.

Her communicator vibrates.

She glances at it.

**MEDICAL BILLING — PAYMENT REMINDER**

She dismisses the notification.

Ari notices the change in her expression but not the message itself.

**ARI**

Everything okay?

**MARA**

Of course. I just remembered the universe expects me to pay for things.

**ARI**

Inconvenient.

**MARA**

Deeply.

She reaches for Ari's untouched toast.

**ARI**

Excuse me?

**MARA**

You weren't eating it.

**ARI**

I was considering it.

**MARA**

Consider faster.

Ari laughs.

Mara looks at the match schedule again.

**MARA**

For what it's worth, I think you've got a chance.

**ARI**

Against Crystal?

**MARA**

No, against the toast. Yes, against Crystal.

**ARI**

Any advice?

**MARA**

Don't try to beat her at being Crystal Devroe.

**ARI**

Meaning?

**MARA**

She's had ten years to perfect that particular skill.

For once, Ari has no immediate reply.

Mara smiles.

**MARA**

See? My professional advice is improving.

---

# SCENE 3 — THE PRICE OF A LEGEND

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 3, LATE AFTERNOON]**

**INT. HALL OF CHAMPIONS**

Crystal stands before a display commemorating the 2097 tournament.

An interviewer finishes a segment.

**INTERVIEWER**

Ten years later, do you still consider that victory the proudest moment of your life?

**CRYSTAL**

One of them.

**INTERVIEWER**

Would you change anything?

Crystal looks at the archival image of herself standing over Nova.

**CRYSTAL**

Quite a few things that happened afterward.

**INTERVIEWER**

Such as?

**CRYSTAL**

That's a longer interview.

The interviewer smiles, thanks her, and departs.

Ari approaches.

**ARI**

You didn't answer the question.

**CRYSTAL**

I answered the part I could in thirty seconds.

**ARI**

You helped make WAR look like it had changed.

**CRYSTAL**

I believed it could.

**ARI**

People believed you.

**CRYSTAL**

Some still do.

**ARI**

My sibling did.

Crystal's expression changes.

**CRYSTAL**

You're from the settlement affected by the evacuation crisis.

**ARI**

You know about it?

**CRYSTAL**

I know the official account.

**ARI**

Then you don't know very much.

Crystal doesn't respond defensively.

**CRYSTAL**

What do you know that I don't?

**ARI**

I know relief was requested before WAR claims it was. I know the transport capacity wasn't exhausted. And I know someone changed the timeline afterward.

**CRYSTAL**

Do you have evidence?

**ARI**

Some.

**CRYSTAL**

Then we should look at it.

Ari seems momentarily surprised.

**ARI**

Just like that?

**CRYSTAL**

Do you want me to refuse?

**ARI**

I want you to understand what happened.

**CRYSTAL**

So do I.

A pause.

**ARI**

You could have done more after 2097.

**CRYSTAL**

Yes.

Ari studies her.

**CRYSTAL**

I don't know whether I could have prevented what happened to your family. I wasn't running WAR, and I'd left its Ganymede position before the crisis.

**ARI**

But you knew what the company was.

**CRYSTAL**

I knew some of it. I learned more. Eventually I realized I couldn't change the rest from the position they'd given me.

**ARI**

And you walked away.

**CRYSTAL**

I did.

**ARI**

People who lived there couldn't.

Crystal absorbs that.

**CRYSTAL**

No. They couldn't.

Silence.

Then she glances toward the arena schedule.

**CRYSTAL**

We fight tomorrow.

**ARI**

I haven't forgotten.

**CRYSTAL**

Good. Don't expect me to go easy on you.

**ARI**

I wouldn't want you to.

**CRYSTAL**

Then we understand each other.

Ari leaves.

Raven emerges from a side corridor.

**RAVEN**

That looked unpleasant.

**CRYSTAL**

It was useful.

**RAVEN**

You seem to have a talent for collecting angry young people.

**CRYSTAL**

And you seem to have a talent for avoiding questions.

Raven says nothing.

**CRYSTAL**

You heard what Ari said?

**RAVEN**

Enough.

**CRYSTAL**

If you know anything about those records—

**RAVEN**

Not everything WAR did passed through me.

**CRYSTAL**

That's not what I asked.

Raven looks at the archival display.

**RAVEN**

No. It isn't.

He leaves before she can continue.

---

# SCENE 4 — A MATTER OF COMPATIBILITY

**[OPTIONAL CINEMATIC]**

**INT. NEURAL RESEARCH WING — DAY 3, EVENING**

Ren is reviewing a simulation while Varga records synchronization data.

Jean-Paul arrives carrying a medical consent form.

**JEAN-PAUL**

Doctor. I was told you needed me.

**VARGA**

For a routine screening.

**JEAN-PAUL**

How disappointing.

**VARGA**

You haven't seen the questionnaire.

Ren looks up.

**REN**

Some of the questions are very unusual.

**VARGA**

Only if you answer honestly.

Jean-Paul smiles.

**JEAN-PAUL**

That seems rather unfair.

Varga gestures toward a chair.

**VARGA**

Sit.

He does.

**JEAN-PAUL**

You have an authoritative manner.

**VARGA**

And you have an inconvenient tendency to talk when someone is trying to measure your attention.

**JEAN-PAUL**

Perhaps you should measure how much attention I'm paying to you.

Varga studies the readout.

**VARGA**

Elevated.

Ren looks between them.

**REN**

Is that an actual result?

**VARGA**

Not one we're publishing.

Ren returns to the simulator, slightly embarrassed.

Jean-Paul leans toward Varga.

**JEAN-PAUL**

You enjoy making people uncomfortable.

**VARGA**

Not particularly.

**JEAN-PAUL**

No?

**VARGA**

I enjoy seeing what they do when they're uncomfortable.

Their eyes meet.

**JEAN-PAUL**

That's worse.

**VARGA**

Scientifically, it's much more useful.

The interaction remains playful, but Jean-Paul notices an encrypted research folder on her console.

**PRIVATE STUDY — AFFECTIVE VARIABILITY**

He says nothing.

Varga notices where he looked.

She closes the window.

**VARGA**

Curiosity is a useful trait.

**JEAN-PAUL**

I thought you'd approve.

**VARGA**

Only when appropriately supervised.

---

# SCENE 5 — THE WRONG WAY TO BEAT A CHAMPION

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 4, BEFORE ARI'S MATCH]**

**INT. PRACTICE FACILITY**

Ari practices against a simulated opponent using archived footage of Crystal's 2097 fighting style.

The simulation repeatedly anticipates Ari's approach.

Ari mutters in frustration.

Mara watches from the doorway.

**MARA**

You're doing it again.

**ARI**

Doing what?

**MARA**

Trying to prove something to a machine that can't appreciate the effort.

**ARI**

I'm studying her reactions.

**MARA**

You're studying how to make the same mistake with increasing precision.

Ari shuts down the simulation.

**ARI**

You have a better idea?

**MARA**

Several. Most of them involve not attacking her where she expects you.

She approaches the controls.

**MARA**

Look at this.

Mara replays Crystal's counterattack.

**MARA**

You see how she shifts her weight before the strike?

**ARI**

Yes.

**MARA**

That's not the important part.

She pauses the image one moment earlier.

**MARA**

Here. She's already decided what you're going to do.

**ARI**

So?

**MARA**

Make her decide wrong.

Ari considers the demonstration.

**ARI**

That's useful.

**MARA**

I know. It's very inconvenient for my reputation.

Ari turns to face her.

**ARI**

Why are you helping me?

Mara considers the question.

**MARA**

Maybe I want to fight someone interesting in the final.

**ARI**

Maybe?

**MARA**

Maybe I like your company.

A small pause.

Ari smiles.

**ARI**

That sounds dangerously close to a compliment.

**MARA**

Don't tell anyone.

### [CHOICE 2 — RESPONSE]

**A. Competitive**

**ARI:** If we meet in the final, I won't hold back.

**MARA:** Good. Neither will I.

**B. Warm**

**ARI:** For what it's worth, I like your company too.

**MARA:** I had a suspicion. You're getting easier to read.

**C. Teasing**

**ARI:** So all this advice is an elaborate attempt to impress me?

**MARA:** It's working, isn't it?

**[All responses converge.]**

An announcement calls Ari to the preparation area.

**MARA**

Go on. Make her work for it.

**ARI**

You coming to watch?

**MARA**

I wouldn't miss it.

---

# SCENE 6 — ARI VS. CRYSTAL

**[MANDATORY FIGHT — DAY 4]**

**INT. MAIN ARENA**

The crowd is enormous.

Crystal's image appears beside archival footage from 2097.

Ari's image appears beside a news report describing the Ganymede dissident movement.

**ALEX (BROADCAST)**

A veteran legend against one of the tournament's most determined newcomers.

**LEENA**

Crystal Devroe has spent ten years hearing people tell her she's unbeatable. Ari Solano may be the first person this week who genuinely doesn't care.

**ALEX**

You think Solano can win?

**LEENA**

I think Crystal's going to have to earn it.

### Pre-fight cinematic

Ari's HAR enters the arena.

Crystal's HAR steps forward.

**CRYSTAL**

You looked at the footage.

**ARI**

I did.

**CRYSTAL**

Then you know what you're getting into.

**ARI**

I know what you used to do.

Crystal smiles faintly.

**CRYSTAL**

Good answer.

The arena lights intensify.

**SHIRRO**

FIGHT!

### [FIGHT 2 — ARI VS. CRYSTAL]

This is an ordinary, fair championship fight.

Crystal is a technically skilled opponent who uses well-timed counters, strong defense, and punishing responses to predictable attacks.

The fight tests what Ari learned from Mara without making that training mandatory for success.

**Default fictional outcome:** Crystal wins.

**Playable outcome:** The actual player result determines the story branch.

---

# SCENE 7A — IF CRYSTAL WINS

**[DEFAULT STORY ROUTE]**

Ari's HAR falls to one knee.

Crystal steps back rather than delivering an unnecessary final strike.

**SHIRRO**

CRYSTAL DEVROE TAKES THE MATCH!

**ALEX**

The legend remains undefeated in Group A!

Ari exits the cockpit, disappointed and frustrated.

Crystal approaches.

**CRYSTAL**

You nearly caught me with that second counter.

**ARI**

Nearly doesn't count.

**CRYSTAL**

In the standings? No.

Ari looks away.

**CRYSTAL**

You fought well.

**ARI**

I wanted to win.

**CRYSTAL**

I noticed.

She pauses.

**CRYSTAL**

Send me those Ganymede records.

Ari looks back.

**ARI**

You're still willing to help?

**CRYSTAL**

I said I would.

**ARI**

Even after all that?

**CRYSTAL**

Ari, I've been insulted by people far more important than you.

Ari almost laughs.

**CRYSTAL**

And some far less interesting.

Her expression softens.

**CRYSTAL**

Winning against me wasn't a condition.

She walks away.

### Default Group A standings

1. Crystal — 2 wins.
2. Ari — 1 win.
3. Selene — 0 wins.

Both Crystal and Ari qualify for the knockout stage.

Under the tournament's published seeding, Ari will face Group B's winner in the quarterfinals.

If Mara wins Group B as expected, that opponent will be Mara.

---

# SCENE 7B — IF ARI WINS

**[ALTERNATE STORY ROUTE]**

Crystal's HAR crashes against the arena floor.

The crowd erupts.

For a moment, the commentators are speechless.

**SHIRRO**

ARI SOLANO HAS DEFEATED CRYSTAL DEVROE!

**ALEX**

THE 2097 CHAMPION HAS FALLEN!

**LEENA**

That changes the entire Group A bracket.

Ari emerges from the HAR, exhilarated.

Crystal approaches.

**CRYSTAL**

Well fought.

**ARI**

I wasn't sure I'd catch you.

**CRYSTAL**

Neither was I.

She smiles.

**CRYSTAL**

You found a weakness I should have corrected years ago.

**ARI**

I had help.

**CRYSTAL**

Then you know the value of listening.

Ari glances toward the competitors' section, where Mara is cheering.

**CRYSTAL**

Don't let winning convince you that you've answered every question.

**ARI**

I won't.

**CRYSTAL**

Good. Now send me those Ganymede records.

Ari looks surprised.

**ARI**

You're still interested?

**CRYSTAL**

More than ever.

She offers a handshake.

Ari accepts.

### Alternate Group A standings

1. Ari — 2 wins.
2. Crystal — 1 win.
3. Selene — 0 wins.

Ari now faces Group B's runner-up in the quarterfinals.

If Group B's default results remain unchanged, this means:

- Ari vs. Ren.
- Crystal vs. Mara.

Ari and Mara will not face each other in the quarterfinals, although they may still meet later if both advance.

**Important:** The scene must not later pretend that Crystal won or that Ari was automatically assigned to fight Mara.

# SCENE 8 — THE PERSON WAITING OUTSIDE

**[CINEMATIC — AFTER THE FIGHT]**

**INT. COMPETITORS' MEDICAL CORRIDOR**

Ari exits a routine post-match examination.

Mara is waiting beside a vending machine.

She holds two drinks.

**MARA**

I got you something.

Ari examines the cup.

**ARI**

What is it?

**MARA**

No idea. The machine called it victory-flavored electrolyte recovery.

**ARI**

That sounds terrible.

**MARA**

It was either that or commemorative Shirro coffee.

She holds up the alternative cup.

**MARA**

Apparently it comes with a speech.

Ari laughs.

### If Ari lost

**MARA**

You gave her a hell of a fight.

**ARI**

I lost.

**MARA**

Yes. I was there.

**ARI**

Then why do you sound so pleased?

**MARA**

Because you made Crystal Devroe change her entire approach halfway through a match.

**ARI**

It wasn't enough.

**MARA**

Today.

Ari looks at her.

**MARA**

You're still in the tournament, you know.

### If Ari won

**MARA**

You actually did it.

**ARI**

You sound surprised.

**MARA**

I was surprised. Crystal's frightening.

**ARI**

Should I be offended?

**MARA**

Only if you think I'm easily impressed.

She hands Ari the drink.

**MARA**

Besides, I told you to make her decide wrong. You listened.

**ARI**

I do that occasionally.

**MARA**

Let's not make a habit of it.

### Shared continuation

Ari takes a sip.

**ARI**

This is awful.

**MARA**

I know. I tried mine first.

**ARI**

You still gave it to me?

**MARA**

I wanted to see your face.

They both laugh.

Then Ari notices that Mara's expression has become thoughtful.

**ARI**

What?

**MARA**

Nothing.

**ARI**

Mara.

She considers saying something more personal, then chooses a lighter answer.

**MARA**

Just wondering whether you're always this stubborn.

**ARI**

Usually.

**MARA**

That could be a problem.

**ARI**

For you?

Mara holds Ari's gaze for a moment.

**MARA**

Possibly.

The announcement system calls the remaining Group B competitors for tomorrow's matches.

Mara looks toward the arena.

**MARA**

I've got Ren tomorrow.

**ARI**

Good luck.

**MARA**

I prefer skill.

**ARI**

Then use both.

She smiles.

**MARA**

I'll consider it.

Mara turns to leave.

**ARI**

Dinner sometime?

She stops.

**MARA**

Is that a request for tactical consultation?

**ARI**

No.

Mara's smile becomes noticeably warmer.

**MARA**

Then ask me again after tomorrow.

She leaves.

Ari watches her go.

The moment is hopeful, but it establishes no guarantee about the tournament or the relationship.

---

# SCENE 9 — THE THINGS THE COMPUTER DIDN'T SAY

**[SHORT MYSTERY SCENE — DAY 4, NIGHT]**

**INT. NEURAL DIAGNOSTICS LAB**

Ren reviews footage from a recent enhanced-link test.

Angel stands at an independent monitoring console.

On the screen, a HAR executes a difficult defensive maneuver.

**REN**

That response was faster than my conscious correction.

**ANGEL**

Predictive assistance?

**REN**

Probably. That's what the system is designed to do.

Ren replays the sequence.

**REN**

Except I don't remember intending the adjustment.

**ANGEL**

You don't necessarily remember every motor intention.

**REN**

I know.

Ren looks again.

**REN**

It still feels strange.

Angel compares the neural trace with the executed movement.

A small discrepancy appears.

**ANGEL**

Do you have an independent recording?

**REN**

Yes.

**ANGEL**

Good. Keep it.

**REN**

Why?

Angel displays a record associated with the obsolete Nova identifier.

**ANGEL**

Because the central system has already corrected its copy.

Ren looks concerned.

**REN**

Corrected what?

**ANGEL**

That's what I'd like to know.

CUT TO:

**INT. PRIVATE RESEARCH OFFICE**

Varga watches a short recording of Steffan talking to Lyra.

She adds a time-stamped note to her private observations.

Her eyes linger on a separate interview schedule.

She moves one appointment by twenty minutes.

The change places Steffan and Lyra in the same backstage corridor the next afternoon.

Varga closes the schedule.

Her office becomes quiet again.

The act is small, nearly mundane.

Its eventual consequences will not be.

---

# SCENE 10 — THE EMPTY PLACE

**[OPTIONAL ARI EPILOGUE — NIGHT]**

**INT. OBSERVATION DECK**

Ari stands alone, looking out toward Ganymede.

A news display shows footage of the championship and Crystal's public statements.

Ari opens an old personal message thread.

The most recent entry is several years old.

A photograph shows Ari with the sibling who died during the crisis.

No sentimental speech is necessary.

Ari starts recording a voice message, then stops.

After a moment, Ari closes the conversation.

A new message appears.

**MARA:** The machine's coffee was worse. In case you're wondering.

Ari smiles.

### [CHOICE 3 — REPLY]

**A.** That's reassuring. I was beginning to question your judgment.

**B.** I'm glad you were there today.

**C.** We should find somewhere that serves actual food.

Mara responds appropriately, with humor or a hint of affection.

Ari puts the communicator away.

The camera pulls back to reveal the vast darkness beyond the observation windows and Ganymede hanging beneath Jupiter.

**END OF CHAPTER 2**

# ARCADE MODE — COMPRESSED VERSION

## Opening transition

A broadcast montage shows Lyra defeating Milano.

**ALEX:** Another spectacular victory for Lyra Kisaragi!

**LEENA:** And tomorrow, Crystal Devroe faces the Ganymede newcomer Ari Solano.

Mara approaches Ari in the competitors' corridor.

**MARA:** You're going up against a legend.

**ARI:** I know.

**MARA:** Good. Then try not to look so impressed.

## Pre-fight

**CRYSTAL:** You've seen the footage. You know what I'm capable of.

**ARI:** I've seen what you used to do.

**CRYSTAL:** Then let's see what you've learned.

**[FIGHT — ARI VS. CRYSTAL]**

## Post-fight, Crystal victory

**CRYSTAL:** Well fought. And send me those Ganymede records.

**ARI:** Even after I lost?

**CRYSTAL:** They weren't part of the wager.

## Post-fight, Ari victory

**CRYSTAL:** You earned that victory. Send me the records.

**ARI:** You still want to help?

**CRYSTAL:** More than ever.

## Closing transition

Mara watches the result.

**MARA:** Not bad, Solano.

Ren notices an unusual response in a neural-interface recording.

**REN:** I don't remember making that adjustment.

Angel studies the log.

**ANGEL:** Then why does the system say you did?

The screen flickers.

**NVA-97 — RECORD UPDATED**

# IMPLEMENTATION NOTES

## Match state

`GROUP_A_RESULT` records the actual Ari–Crystal result.

| Result | Group winner | Ari quarterfinal opponent if Group B follows default results |
|---|---|---|
| Crystal wins | Crystal | Mara |
| Ari wins | Ari | Ren |

This result must propagate to knockout pairings, announcements, subsequent character scenes, and match commentary.

## Relationship state

`MARA_RAPPORT` accumulates small differences in dialogue tone without making early flirting mandatory for romance.

`MARA_TRUST` is tracked separately and becomes important when Ari receives WAR's advanced neural-program invitation.

The romance must not depend solely on selecting flirtatious dialogue.

## Narrative information

`GANYMEDE_EVIDENCE_STAGE` remains preliminary.

Ari has contradictions and incomplete records, not yet definitive evidence of corporate responsibility.

`NOVA_ANOMALY_STAGE` advances through Angel and Ren's independent investigation.

The player has not yet discovered Kreissack's survival.

`VARGA_OBSERVATION_STAGE` now includes social scheduling interference, but no major public manipulation is revealed.

## Character continuity

Lyra has not yet cheated on or broken up with Dante.

Steffan's attraction is partly sincere and partly driven by rivalry.

Dante's anger must remain understandable without reducing him to a stereotypical jealous boyfriend.

Crystal is not responsible for the Ganymede crisis merely because she once represented WAR's interests.

Mara's medical-debt problem is hinted at, not fully disclosed.

## Scene priority

**Mandatory cinematic content:**

- Lyra's victory and Steffan's pursuit.
- Ari–Mara cafeteria interaction.
- Ari–Crystal confrontation.
- Ari–Crystal match.
- Ari–Mara post-match encounter.
- Brief neural-anomaly scene.

**Optional expanded content:**

- Jean-Paul–Varga screening.
- Full pre-match practice conversation.
- Ari's late-night observation-deck scene.

**Player-controlled content:**

- Ari vs. Crystal.
- Optional training drill.
- Dialogue choices that influence relationship tone.

## Success criteria

By the chapter's end:

1. The player respects Crystal as a formidable opponent and a complicated person.
2. Ari's political grievance feels specific rather than purely rhetorical.
3. Ari and Mara have developed credible mutual attraction.
4. Lyra is firmly established as a skilled athlete with an identity beyond her romantic relationships.
5. Steffan's pursuit is attractive but contains visible competitive motives.
6. Neural enhancement appears exciting and slightly unsettling, not obviously evil.
7. Both outcomes of Ari–Crystal remain narratively valid.

---

# NEXT CHAPTER — THE TROUBLE WITH ATTRACTION

Chapter 3 should cover Days 5–7 and the conclusion of qualification.

**Principal developments:**

- Mara defeats Ren and wins Group B.
- Steffan defeats Lyra, securing Group C's top position.
- Angel defeats Dante, winning Group D.
- Lyra's interest in Steffan becomes harder to dismiss.
- Ari and Mara have a more personal conversation about family, money, and the future.
- Jean-Paul deepens his involvement with Varga.
- Ari receives stronger evidence about Ganymede.
- The advanced neural-program invitation is introduced.
- Ari and Mara become lovers before the final knockout bracket is announced.

The ending then diverges according to Ari's Group A position:

- **Default:** Ari draws Mara in the quarterfinals.
- **Alternate:** Ari draws Ren, while Crystal faces Mara.

The romantic stakes remain significant in both routes, but their immediate competitive consequences differ.

# ONE MUST FALL 2107
## Chapter 3 — The Trouble with Attraction

**Screenplay:** v0.1  
**Timeline:** Tournament Days 5–7  
**Player character:** Ari Solano  
**Mandatory championship fights:** None; concluding qualification matches are shown through broadcasts  
**Optional gameplay:** Ari vs. Mara, practice sparring  
**Chapter climax:** Ari and Mara become lovers; the knockout bracket is about to be revealed

### Dramatic objectives

1. Complete the group-stage standings consistently with the revised tournament bracket.
2. Make Ari and Mara's developing love feel earned.
3. Establish the difference between Steffan's genuine attraction to Lyra and his desire to humiliate Dante.
4. Show Lyra choosing Steffan and accepting responsibility for hurting Dante.
5. Make Varga's intervention meaningful but limited.
6. Introduce evidence of deliberate delay in Ganymede's emergency response.
7. Show both the promise and the disturbing anomalies of advanced neural control.
8. End with Ari facing a choice between personal trust, competitive advantage, and institutional access.

---

# SCENE 1 — A SMALL MISTAKE

**[BROADCAST CINEMATIC — DAY 5, MORNING]**

**INT. CHAMPIONSHIP ARENA**

Ren Akiyama's Helix stands opposite Mara Voss's battered Flail.

The enormous display shows their records.

**MARA VOSS — 1 WIN**

**REN AKIYAMA — 1 WIN**

Both have already defeated Raven.

Whoever wins takes first place in Group B.

**ALEX (BROADCAST)**

One of these pilots is going straight into the knockout stage as a group winner.

**LEENA**

And we have a fascinating contrast. Ren has some of the best recorded reaction times in the tournament. Mara has spent years fighting people who were faster, stronger, and better equipped.

**ALEX**

You're suggesting experience might beat technology?

**LEENA**

I'm suggesting Mara knows what to do when it does.

The fight begins.

Ren moves with remarkable precision. Helix anticipates Flail's opening attacks and counters with minimal wasted motion.

Mara changes tactics, repeatedly presenting awkward, imperfect openings.

Ren responds flawlessly to the first two.

On the third, Helix begins a counterattack before Mara has completed the feint.

Its movement is fast—almost too fast.

Mara abruptly reverses direction and catches the robot off balance.

Flail lands the decisive blow.

**SHIRRO**

MARA VOSS TAKES GROUP B!

The crowd cheers.

**[POST-FIGHT — COMPETITORS' AREA]**

Ren approaches Mara.

**REN**

That last feint was extraordinary.

**MARA**

Thanks. I was hoping you'd fall for it.

**REN**

I didn't.

**MARA**

Your robot did.

Ren hesitates.

**REN**

That's the strange part.

**MARA**

What is?

**REN**

I remember deciding to wait.

Mara looks back toward Helix.

**MARA**

Well, whoever decided to attack owes you an apology.

Ren laughs, though the expression doesn't quite reach their eyes.

Across the room, Ari watches the exchange.

For the moment, it seems like a curious malfunction rather than evidence of anything sinister.

---

# SCENE 2 — A MATCH WORTH WATCHING

**[BROADCAST CINEMATIC — DAY 5, AFTERNOON]**

**INT. MAIN ARENA**

Lyra's fans have transformed the stadium into a sea of illuminated Electra symbols.

The broadcast screens show her victories, modeling campaigns, and training footage.

**ALEX**

Lyra Kisaragi and Steffan Thomas. Both undefeated in Group C.

**LEENA**

Both exceptional competitors. And given their recent conversations, I suspect neither intends to let the other forget the result.

**ALEX**

You mean the flirting?

**LEENA**

I mean the fighting, Alex.

In the preparation corridor, Dante adjusts Lyra's wrist guard.

**DANTE**

Thomas likes to bait people into reckless attacks. Don't give him what he wants.

**LYRA**

I've seen his fights.

**DANTE**

I know. I'm just saying—

**LYRA**

Dante.

He looks at her.

**LYRA**

I can beat him.

Dante pauses, then smiles.

**DANTE**

I know you can.

He kisses her forehead.

**DANTE**

Make him work.

**LYRA**

I intend to.

She enters the arena.

Steffan's Pyros advances toward Electra.

### Pre-fight dialogue

**STEFFAN**

You've got an impressive following.

**LYRA**

They have excellent taste.

**STEFFAN**

I wonder how they'll react when I win.

**LYRA**

Probably better than you will when you lose.

**STEFFAN**

Shall we find out?

**LYRA**

Gladly.

### [NONPLAYER QUALIFYING MATCH]

The fight is fast, technical, and competitive.

Lyra repeatedly counters Pyros's pressure, using Electra's speed to control distance.

Steffan struggles initially.

He then changes rhythm, baiting Lyra into a commitment he has been preparing for.

He wins through a legitimate tactical adjustment, not because Lyra is distracted by attraction.

**SHIRRO**

STEFFAN THOMAS WINS GROUP C!

The crowd is loud but divided.

Lyra removes her helmet.

She's frustrated, but not devastated.

**[POST-FIGHT]**

Steffan approaches her.

**STEFFAN**

You nearly had me.

**LYRA**

I know.

**STEFFAN**

That second reversal—

**LYRA**

You were baiting the third.

**STEFFAN**

And you noticed.

**LYRA**

Too late.

Steffan smiles.

**STEFFAN**

There's a difference between noticing too late and not noticing at all.

**LYRA**

There's also a difference between being gracious and being unbearable.

**STEFFAN**

Which am I?

**LYRA**

Still evaluating.

She begins to leave.

**STEFFAN**

You didn't answer about dinner.

Lyra stops.

**LYRA**

I told you I'm with Dante.

**STEFFAN**

That's not what I asked.

Her expression becomes more serious.

**LYRA**

And that's precisely why you're dangerous.

**STEFFAN**

Only in the arena.

**LYRA**

You're a terrible liar.

Steffan watches her walk away.

For the first time, his expression suggests something more than the satisfaction of winning.

---

# SCENE 3 — THE MAN IN THE OTHER CORNER

**[OPTIONAL CINEMATIC — DAY 5, EVENING]**

**INT. COMPETITORS' BAR**

Dante sits alone with a glass of water.

Steffan enters, still buoyant from his victory.

He stops beside Dante.

**STEFFAN**

I expected a congratulation.

**DANTE**

Try expecting less.

**STEFFAN**

That's an interesting philosophy for a championship contender.

**DANTE**

You won a qualifying match.

**STEFFAN**

Against your girlfriend.

Dante slowly turns.

**DANTE**

Careful.

Steffan's smile remains, but he doesn't retreat.

**STEFFAN**

Does it bother you that she likes talking to me?

**DANTE**

It bothers me that you think everything is a contest.

**STEFFAN**

Coming from you, that's almost poetic.

Dante stands.

For a moment, the two are very close.

**DANTE**

You don't know her.

**STEFFAN**

Then perhaps you should stop worrying about me.

Dante studies him.

**DANTE**

I know what you do when you can't beat someone where it matters.

Steffan's expression tightens.

**STEFFAN**

And where does it matter, Dante?

The question hangs between them.

Dante looks away first.

**DANTE**

We'll settle it in the arena.

**STEFFAN**

I certainly hope so.

Steffan leaves.

Dante remains standing, more troubled than he wants to admit.

---

# SCENE 4 — THE HOLD ORDER

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 6, MORNING]**

**INT. QUIET CAFÉ — TOURNAMENT COMPLEX**

Ari sits opposite Crystal.

The table is covered with a few documents and a handheld display.

Their conversation begins professionally, despite the awkwardness remaining from their match.

**CRYSTAL**

You said the emergency request was earlier than the public report claimed.

**ARI**

Seven hours earlier.

Ari activates the display.

Two timelines appear.

**PUBLIC INCIDENT REPORT:** Relief transport unavailable.

**INTERNAL CARGO LOG:** Emergency cargo prepared. Departure authorization pending.

Crystal examines the records.

**CRYSTAL**

Where did you get this?

**ARI**

Someone who was there.

**CRYSTAL**

Someone willing to testify?

**ARI**

Not yet.

**CRYSTAL**

Then we have to be careful.

**ARI**

The log is real.

**CRYSTAL**

It may be. But a departure hold isn't automatically proof of why someone ordered it.

**ARI**

They knew people were dying.

**CRYSTAL**

I believe you.

Ari looks up, surprised.

**CRYSTAL**

Believing you and proving who made the decision are two different problems.

She gestures to the screen.

**CRYSTAL**

Here. The authorization reference.

Ari enlarges the field.

**CRYSTAL**

This was routed through commercial operations, not emergency response.

**ARI**

Meaning?

**CRYSTAL**

Meaning somebody may have treated a relief shipment as part of a negotiation.

Ari stares at the screen.

**ARI**

That's what I've been saying for years.

**CRYSTAL**

Then now we have something specific to investigate.

Ari leans back.

**ARI**

You know what the worst part is?

Crystal waits.

**ARI**

I used to defend WAR. I thought they were building something important out there.

**CRYSTAL**

They did build important things.

**ARI**

And then they decided those things mattered more than the people living among them.

Crystal doesn't argue.

**CRYSTAL**

We'll need the authorization chain and an independent copy of the shipping record.

**ARI**

Can you get them?

**CRYSTAL**

Not by walking into headquarters and asking politely.

Ari almost smiles.

**ARI**

You make it sound like you've tried.

**CRYSTAL**

I have. It was surprisingly ineffective.

They share a brief, reluctant laugh.

**CRYSTAL**

I'll contact someone I trust.

She closes the display.

**CRYSTAL**

And Ari?

**ARI**

Yes?

**CRYSTAL**

Don't send the only copy of that record to anyone at WAR.

Ari nods.

**[CONTINUITY]** This proves a discrepancy and an unusual authorization path, not yet the full motive or the responsible officials. Those are established in Act IV.

---

# SCENE 5 — NOT AN EXHIBITION

**[CINEMATIC WITH OPTIONAL SPARRING — DAY 6, AFTERNOON]**

**INT. PRACTICE ARENA**

Mara is testing a replacement joint on her Flail.

Ari arrives carrying two drinks.

**ARI**

I found something that isn't sold by the same vending machine.

**MARA**

You remembered.

**ARI**

I'm capable of learning.

**MARA**

I've been collecting evidence.

She takes a drink.

**MARA**

How'd your meeting go?

**ARI**

Better than I expected.

**MARA**

With Crystal?

**ARI**

She thinks we might be able to trace the shipping authorization.

**MARA**

Good.

Mara notices the tension in Ari's face.

**MARA**

That's the expression again.

**ARI**

Which expression?

**MARA**

The one that suggests you're about to declare war on a spreadsheet.

Ari laughs.

**ARI**

It's just frustrating. Knowing something happened and not being able to prove it.

**MARA**

I know a thing or two about problems that don't care how angry you are.

She gestures toward the practice arena.

**MARA**

Want to hit something?

**ARI**

Is that your solution to everything?

**MARA**

No. Sometimes I eat.

### [OPTIONAL GAMEPLAY — PRACTICE SPAR]

A single friendly practice round between Ari and Mara.

There is no tournament consequence.

**If Ari wins:**

**MARA**

Not bad. I may have created a problem for myself.

**ARI**

You could stop giving me advice.

**MARA**

And miss the chance to complain when you ignore it?

**If Mara wins:**

**MARA**

There. Feel better?

**ARI**

Not particularly.

**MARA**

Then again. Until you do.

**[Shared continuation]**

The machines power down.

Mara stretches her shoulders.

Ari notices that her wrist communicator is displaying a message from a medical billing service.

She closes it.

**ARI**

You mentioned something at home the other day.

Mara's expression becomes guarded.

**MARA**

I did?

**ARI**

You don't have to tell me.

A pause.

**MARA**

My mother needs ongoing treatment. The bills have been... creative.

**ARI**

Is she all right?

**MARA**

Most days. She's tougher than I am, which is frankly insulting.

Ari smiles faintly.

**MARA**

The treatment helps. Paying for it is the part nobody puts in the inspirational advertisements.

**ARI**

Is that why you're competing?

**MARA**

Partly. I also happen to enjoy fighting giant robots.

She takes a breath.

**MARA**

I've been working for years. Sometimes I get ahead. Then another bill arrives, or the equipment fails, or something else breaks.

**ARI**

That sounds exhausting.

**MARA**

It is.

Ari considers offering help, then chooses a different question.

**ARI**

What does your mother think about the tournament?

Mara brightens.

**MARA**

She thinks I should stop getting hit in the head.

**ARI**

A reasonable position.

**MARA**

She also thinks I could win. So she's not entirely consistent.

Ari laughs.

Mara looks toward the practice arena.

**MARA**

If I win this thing, maybe I get to spend a few years solving problems that aren't emergencies.

**ARI**

I hope you do.

Mara glances at Ari.

**MARA**

Careful. That almost sounded sincere.

**ARI**

It was.

For a moment, Mara has no joke prepared.

**MARA**

Well. That's inconvenient.

The moment is interrupted by an arena announcement.

**ANNOUNCER**

Final Group D qualifying match begins in fifteen minutes.

Mara picks up her equipment bag.

**MARA**

Come on. Let's see if Dante can stay angry long enough to win.

---

# SCENE 6 — THE QUIETEST VICTORY

**[BROADCAST CINEMATIC — DAY 6, EVENING]**

**INT. MAIN ARENA**

Angel's Chronos faces Dante's Shredder.

Dante fights aggressively and with remarkable power.

Angel patiently exploits his commitment to attacks.

In the final exchange, Dante attempts a decisive assault.

Angel anticipates it and wins with a precise counter.

**SHIRRO**

ANGEL TAKES GROUP D!

**ALEX**

A remarkable finish! Dante advances, but he'll enter the knockout bracket as the runner-up.

**LEENA**

And that means he'll be facing a group winner. Depending on the bracket, that could include Steffan Thomas.

The camera catches Dante hearing the announcement.

His disappointment gives way to a fierce, almost eager expression.

**[BACKSTAGE]**

Angel reviews an equipment report.

Ren approaches.

**REN**

Congratulations.

**ANGEL**

Thank you.

**REN**

You made that final counter look effortless.

**ANGEL**

It wasn't.

**REN**

You didn't use the advanced predictive package?

**ANGEL**

No.

**REN**

Why not?

Angel turns the display toward Ren.

It shows a mismatch between an experimental system's intended command trace and its recorded output.

**ANGEL**

Because I'd rather know which decisions are mine.

Ren studies the trace.

**REN**

That's an unfair way of putting it.

**ANGEL**

Possibly.

**REN**

The system can anticipate motor commands before conscious awareness. That's part of the research.

**ANGEL**

Then why did the recording change afterward?

Ren looks again.

The question is more difficult to dismiss.

**[CONTINUITY]** These anomalies concern advanced predictive assistance and record manipulation. They do not establish that Ren has been deeply integrated into Nova.

---

# SCENE 7 — AN UNFORTUNATE COINCIDENCE

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 6, NIGHT]**

**INT. BACKSTAGE SERVICE CORRIDOR**

Lyra leaves a late sponsor interview.

Her communicator shows a message from Dante.

**DANTE:** Still at the arena. Dinner?

She begins typing a reply.

A door opens.

Steffan emerges from a training room.

**STEFFAN**

You're still here.

**LYRA**

So are you.

**STEFFAN**

I couldn't sleep.

**LYRA**

You should try being less pleased with yourself.

**STEFFAN**

I've been told it's incurable.

She smiles.

**STEFFAN**

That was a good match yesterday.

**LYRA**

You already said that.

**STEFFAN**

I meant it both times.

They walk together toward the observation corridor.

**LYRA**

You know, you're much easier to tolerate when you're not performing for an audience.

**STEFFAN**

That's unfortunate. I rather enjoy audiences.

**LYRA**

I've noticed.

He stops beside a window.

Jupiter glows beyond the glass.

**STEFFAN**

Do you ever get tired of it?

**LYRA**

Of what?

**STEFFAN**

Being watched.

She looks at him.

**LYRA**

Sometimes.

**STEFFAN**

I thought you loved it.

**LYRA**

I love performing. That's not the same thing as wanting everyone to own a piece of me.

Steffan studies her.

For a moment, the usual joke doesn't come.

**STEFFAN**

I hadn't thought about it that way.

**LYRA**

There are lots of things you haven't thought about.

**STEFFAN**

You could educate me.

**LYRA**

That sounds like a full-time job.

They laugh.

The conversation becomes quieter.

**STEFFAN**

I wanted to win against you.

**LYRA**

Obviously.

**STEFFAN**

Not only because of Dante.

Lyra's expression changes.

**LYRA**

You understand how little that helps?

**STEFFAN**

Yes.

**LYRA**

Do you?

Steffan moves slightly closer but does not touch her.

**STEFFAN**

I like you, Lyra.

She looks at him for a long moment.

**LYRA**

You make it very difficult to do the sensible thing.

**STEFFAN**

I've heard that too.

This time she doesn't laugh.

Lyra closes the distance and kisses him.

Steffan responds.

The kiss is affectionate, impulsive, and clearly mutual.

Afterward, Lyra steps back.

**LYRA**

I shouldn't have done that.

**STEFFAN**

I'm not going to pretend I regret it.

**LYRA**

That isn't what I asked.

A pause.

**LYRA**

I need to talk to Dante.

Steffan nods.

**STEFFAN**

I know.

**LYRA**

And I need you not to make this into another victory.

That unsettles him.

**STEFFAN**

Lyra—

**LYRA**

I'm serious.

**STEFFAN**

So am I.

She studies his face, uncertain how much to believe.

**LYRA**

We'll see.

She leaves.

Steffan stays beside the window.

His excitement is genuine.

So is the satisfaction he feels at having defeated Dante in another arena.

He has not yet learned how to separate the two.

### A brief silent continuation

**INT. VARGA'S OFFICE**

Varga studies a calendar of pilot interviews.

Earlier, she moved Steffan's training appointment and Lyra's sponsor interview into adjacent time slots.

She has no recording of the private kiss.

She does see that both entered the observation corridor within minutes of each other.

She adds a time stamp to her research notes.

**[TECHNICAL]** Varga created an opportunity for an encounter. She did not guarantee a romantic outcome or secretly induce the kiss.

---

# SCENE 8 — THE BREAKUP

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 7, MORNING]**

**INT. COMPETITORS' RESIDENTIAL LOUNGE**

Dante is preparing to leave for training when Lyra arrives.

He smiles, relieved to see her.

**DANTE**

There you are. I thought we'd have breakfast.

**LYRA**

Dante, we need to talk.

His expression changes.

**DANTE**

About Thomas?

Lyra stops.

**LYRA**

Yes.

Dante turns away briefly.

**DANTE**

I knew he was trying something.

**LYRA**

This isn't just about what he wants.

**DANTE**

What's that supposed to mean?

**LYRA**

I've been attracted to him. I should have been honest about that earlier.

Dante looks back at her.

**DANTE**

Did something happen?

Lyra doesn't evade the question.

**LYRA**

I kissed him last night.

Silence.

**DANTE**

You kissed him.

**LYRA**

Yes.

**DANTE**

After everything he said to me.

**LYRA**

I'm sorry I hurt you.

**DANTE**

You knew what he was doing.

**LYRA**

I know what he was trying to do. I also know what I chose.

Dante struggles with that distinction.

**DANTE**

So what happens now?

**LYRA**

I don't want to keep pretending things are the same between us.

**DANTE**

You're leaving me for him.

**LYRA**

I'm ending our relationship because I don't want to lie to you anymore.

**DANTE**

That's a very elegant way of saying it.

Lyra accepts the criticism.

**LYRA**

Maybe. But it's true.

Dante looks away.

**DANTE**

And you think he's serious?

**LYRA**

I don't know.

He laughs without humor.

**DANTE**

At least one of us is being honest.

**LYRA**

I should have been honest sooner.

She waits, but Dante says nothing.

**LYRA**

You deserve some space.

She turns to leave.

**DANTE**

Lyra.

She stops.

**DANTE**

I really wanted us to win this thing.

Her expression softens.

**LYRA**

We both still can.

**DANTE**

That's not what I meant.

**LYRA**

I know.

She leaves.

Dante remains alone.

The scene stays with him long enough to register genuine heartbreak—not merely a bruised competitive ego.

**[REVISION — CORRIDOR CONTINUATION, Repair Pack R06]** The private lounge conversation above is not recorded. It continues in a public service corridor, where a conventional safety camera has a clear view.

**INT. SERVICE CORRIDOR OUTSIDE THE LOUNGE**

Dante catches up with Lyra. He is angry but not threatening.

**DANTE**

> Is he really worth more to you than everything we built?

**LYRA**

> This isn't about worth. The decision was mine.

She walks on. Dante stays, visibly strained.

A corridor safety camera records only these few lines and their body language. Neither the kiss nor the full breakup conversation is on tape.

---

# SCENE 9 — THE DOCTOR'S APPOINTMENT

**[OPTIONAL CINEMATIC — DAY 7, AFTERNOON]**

**INT. RESEARCH DIVISION — PRIVATE CONSULTATION ROOM**

Jean-Paul arrives for a scheduled follow-up with Varga.

On the table are two cups of tea rather than medical instruments.

**JEAN-PAUL**

How disappointing. I dressed for an examination.

**VARGA**

I assumed you'd prefer to remain conscious.

**JEAN-PAUL**

You're developing a suspicious familiarity with my preferences.

**VARGA**

Occupational hazard.

He sits opposite her.

**JEAN-PAUL**

You moved my appointment twice.

**VARGA**

Scheduling difficulties.

**JEAN-PAUL**

And yet our schedules aligned so beautifully.

**VARGA**

Perhaps fate is less inefficient than I thought.

**JEAN-PAUL**

I suspect fate has access to your calendar.

Varga smiles.

**VARGA**

Do you resent being invited?

**JEAN-PAUL**

Not at all. I simply prefer to know whether I'm attending an appointment or participating in an experiment.

She watches him carefully.

**VARGA**

Why assume those are mutually exclusive?

Jean-Paul smiles, but the reply confirms a suspicion.

**JEAN-PAUL**

And what would you be measuring?

**VARGA**

That depends on what you're willing to reveal.

**JEAN-PAUL**

I might surprise you.

**VARGA**

I would enjoy that.

A notification appears on her console.

**AFFILIATION / COMPETITIVE ANTAGONISM — FOLLOW-UP REVIEW**

Jean-Paul sees the words before she dismisses the notification.

He doesn't mention them.

**JEAN-PAUL**

Tell me something, Ilse.

**VARGA**

Yes?

**JEAN-PAUL**

Do you ever meet anyone you don't want to understand?

She considers the question.

**VARGA**

Occasionally.

**JEAN-PAUL**

And what do you do then?

**VARGA**

I usually invite them back.

They exchange a look.

Their mutual attraction is becoming harder to maintain as a purely professional game.

---

# SCENE 10 — EVERYBODY'S BUSINESS

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 7, AFTERNOON]**

**INT. ARENA PRESS ATRIUM**

A celebrity news channel broadcasts an edited excerpt of Dante and Lyra's breakup.

The recording is from the service-corridor safety camera shown at the end of Scene 8. It captures only the short, strained exchange between Dante and Lyra, without the private conversation that preceded it.

**NEWS PRESENTER**

A dramatic split between two of the tournament's biggest stars, with Steffan Thomas reportedly at the center of the controversy!

The clip makes Dante appear more aggressive and Lyra more dismissive than they were in the full conversation.

Crowds begin discussing the scandal.

**[BACKSTAGE]**

Lyra watches the broadcast with obvious anger.

Ren stands beside her.

**REN**

That's not a fair representation of what happened.

**LYRA**

No. It isn't.

**REN**

Do you know who released it?

**LYRA**

Not yet.

A publicist approaches.

**PUBLICIST**

Lyra, we can arrange an exclusive interview. Your followers are demanding a response.

**LYRA**

My followers can wait.

**PUBLICIST**

It would help manage the story.

**LYRA**

I'm not interested in managing a story somebody else stole from me.

The publicist hesitates.

**LYRA**

And cancel the interview with Steffan.

**PUBLICIST**

But you're both scheduled—

**LYRA**

Then he'll have an opportunity to practice talking without me.

The publicist leaves.

Ren looks impressed.

**REN**

You handled that well.

**LYRA**

I wanted to throw the screen through a wall.

**REN**

That might have been less effective.

**LYRA**

You think?

**REN**

The wall probably belongs to WAR.

Lyra bursts into laughter.

**LYRA**

Ren, I adore you.

Ren looks briefly startled.

**REN**

Thank you?

The scene ends on a warm moment of friendship.

### Separate short scene

**INT. PRODUCTION SUITE**

Shirro watches the scandal coverage.

**SHIRRO**

Well, that's going to sell tickets.

Leena gives him a sharp look.

**LEENA**

It was a private conversation.

**SHIRRO**

And now it's a very public conversation.

**LEENA**

You don't seem curious about who leaked it.

Shirro pauses.

**SHIRRO**

Should I be?

**LEENA**

Probably.

### Hidden causal layer

Varga had inappropriate access to selected building-camera clips through a research data liaison and routed the corridor clip to a media intermediary. Shirro's production team edited it for sensationalism independently. (Jean-Paul's later discovery chain: irregular participant schedules → concealed affective data correlation → camera access log → Varga's intervention timestamps → disclosure to Lyra.)

The media's additional editing and sensationalism were independent decisions.

The full extent of Varga's role will not be revealed until later.

---

# SCENE 11 — SOMETHING WORTH WINNING FOR

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 7, EVENING]**

**INT. ANNIVERSARY COMPLEX — OBSERVATION GARDEN**

A quiet garden occupies a sheltered section of the orbital complex.

Small trees and flowering plants grow beneath carefully regulated artificial light.

Jupiter dominates the view beyond the glass.

Ari arrives to find Mara sitting beside a small ornamental pool.

**MARA**

You're late.

**ARI**

Two minutes.

**MARA**

I was beginning to think you'd escaped.

**ARI**

From dinner?

**MARA**

It's a perfectly reasonable response to the food here.

Ari sits beside her.

A small meal is laid out on the bench.

**ARI**

You found somewhere that doesn't serve commemorative coffee.

**MARA**

I have many talents.

They begin eating.

For a while, the conversation is light.

Mara tells an absurd story about a poorly maintained underground fighting venue.

Ari describes a childhood adventure on Ganymede involving a broken maintenance rover and an extremely unhappy security officer.

They laugh.

Eventually, the conversation becomes quieter.

**MARA**

You don't talk much about your family.

Ari looks at the pool.

**ARI**

There's not much left to say.

**MARA**

You don't have to say anything.

Ari considers that.

**ARI**

My sibling believed in WAR more than I did.

**MARA**

Really?

**ARI**

They thought the company would bring opportunities to the colonies. Jobs. Infrastructure. A future.

A pause.

**ARI**

I used to tease them for being so optimistic.

**MARA**

And then?

**ARI**

When the emergency happened, they kept saying the ships would come. That somebody would authorize them.

Mara listens.

**ARI**

They were right about the ships. The ships existed.

Ari looks away.

**ARI**

They just didn't come.

Silence.

Mara doesn't rush to fill it.

**MARA**

I'm sorry.

Ari nods.

**ARI**

Sometimes I think if I can prove what happened, something will finally make sense.

**MARA**

Maybe it will.

She looks toward Jupiter.

**MARA**

But you should get to have a life while you're doing it.

Ari turns.

**ARI**

That's something you sound very certain about.

**MARA**

I have a lot of opinions.

**ARI**

I've noticed.

They smile.

**MARA**

I spent most of my twenties moving from one contract to another. Repair bills, treatment bills, emergency jobs.

**ARI**

And now you're here.

**MARA**

Now I'm trying to make enough money that I can stop pretending everything is temporary.

She glances at Ari.

**MARA**

Which probably sounds terribly romantic.

**ARI**

It sounds like you care about your mother.

**MARA**

I do.

Ari takes her hand.

Mara looks down, surprised by the gentleness of the gesture.

Then she closes her fingers around Ari's.

**MARA**

You've become rather difficult to make fun of.

**ARI**

That sounds serious.

**MARA**

It is. I may need professional help.

Ari laughs.

The atmosphere shifts.

For several seconds, neither looks away.

### [CHOICE 4 — ARI'S RESPONSE]

**A. Direct**

**ARI:** I've wanted to kiss you since the gala.

**MARA:** You have excellent timing. It only took you a week to mention it.

**B. Playful**

**ARI:** Is this the part where you charge me for your company?

**MARA:** No. This one's on me.

**C. Vulnerable**

**ARI:** I wasn't expecting to feel this close to anyone here.

**MARA:** Neither was I.

**[Shared continuation]**

**[REVISION — ROMANCE CHOICE, Repair Pack R04]** Placed just before the first kiss.

**MARA**

> You know we're both still in the tournament.

**ARI**

> I know.

**MARA**

> And this may become complicated.

### [CHOICE 5 — RECIPROCATE OR REMAIN FRIENDS]

**A. Reciprocate** — `ari_mara = lovers`

**ARI:** I'd still like to find out where this goes.

**MARA:** Good. So would I.

*(Continues below.)*

**B. Remain friends** — `ari_mara = friends`

**ARI:** You're important to me. But I don't want to start a romance I may not be ready for.

**MARA:** I appreciate you telling me plainly.

They share a quiet, affectionate conversation under the Jupiter lights and walk back together. Mara is not hurt or vindictive; the quarterfinal stakes (friendship vs. prize money, fair fight vs. concern) stay serious. *(Skip to Scene 12; later Mara/Ari lines use the friendship variants.)*

**[Branch A continues]**

They kiss.

The moment is warm, unhurried, and mutual.

When they separate, Mara is smiling.

**MARA**

Well.

**ARI**

Well?

**MARA**

I was going to say something clever.

**ARI**

What happened?

**MARA**

You distracted me.

Ari kisses her again.

The camera shifts toward Jupiter and the lights of the garden.

**[TRANSITION — LATER]**

They walk together through a quiet residential corridor, still teasing each other.

At Mara's door, she turns.

**MARA**

You know we're both still in the tournament.

**ARI**

I know.

**MARA**

And I'm not going easy on you if we meet.

**ARI**

I wouldn't ask you to.

She studies Ari for a moment.

**MARA**

Good.

Mara opens the door and takes Ari's hand.

**MARA**

Coming in?

Ari smiles and follows.

The door closes.

**FADE OUT.**

The scene implies intimacy without explicit sexual content.

**[RELATIONSHIP STATE]** Choice 5 sets `ari_mara` to `lovers` or `friends`. Earlier tone choices affect delivery only. `ari_mara_trust` is tracked separately.

# SCENE 12 — THE OFFER

**[CINEMATIC — DAY 7, LATE NIGHT]**

**INT. ARI'S PRIVATE QUARTERS**

Ari returns to the room.

The communicator displays a formal message.

**WAR NEURAL INTEGRATION INITIATIVE**

**PERSONAL INVITATION — ADVANCED PILOT SYNCHRONIZATION PROGRAM**

A short recorded message begins.

**HALDANE (RECORDING)**

Ari Solano. Your qualifying performance has drawn the attention of our research division.

We are inviting a limited group of pilots to participate in advanced neural synchronization trials during the knockout stage.

The program offers specialized technical support, research compensation, and opportunities to work directly with our development team.

Participation is voluntary, subject to a medical evaluation and informed consent.

Ari pauses the recording.

Additional information appears:

**Experimental neural interface.**

**Enhanced response prediction.**

**Potential sensory and cognitive side effects.**

**Confidential research participation agreement.**

Ari scrolls to a final paragraph.

**INSTITUTIONAL LIAISON:** Participating pilots may request meetings with relevant WAR departments through the research office. Access to unrelated corporate records is not guaranteed.

Ari considers the wording.

The offer could provide useful contacts and resources for the Ganymede investigation.

It could also create a new dependence on WAR.

Ari closes the message without responding.

**[TECHNICAL]** The decision to accept or refuse is reserved for Act III, after the knockout bracket is announced. Both options must remain valid.

# IMPLEMENTATION AND CONTINUITY NOTES

## A. Definitive group-stage results

| Group | Result 1 | Result 2 | Result 3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| A | Ari defeats Selene | Crystal defeats Selene | Ari vs. Crystal: player-dependent |
| B | Mara defeats Raven | Ren defeats Raven | Mara defeats Ren |
| C | Steffan defeats Milano | Lyra defeats Milano | Steffan defeats Lyra |
| D | Dante defeats Jean-Paul | Angel defeats Jean-Paul | Angel defeats Dante |

Default Group A winner: Crystal. Alternate: Ari.

Other group standings remain unchanged in Ari's route.

## B. Bracket integrity

With the established seeding convention:

- Group A winner faces Group B runner-up.
- Group A runner-up faces Group B winner.
- Group C winner faces Group D runner-up.
- Group D winner faces Group C runner-up.

The semifinals pair the two A/B quarterfinal winners and the two C/D quarterfinal winners.

Consequently, if Ari wins Group A, a later Ari–Mara meeting is possible in the semifinal, not the quarterfinal.

No match is secretly reassigned merely to maximize romantic drama.

## C. Romance continuity

At the beginning of the chapter:

- Dante and Lyra are together.
- Steffan is actively pursuing Lyra.
- Ari and Mara are attracted to each other but not yet lovers.

By the end:

- Lyra has ended her relationship with Dante and begun exploring a relationship with Steffan.
- Dante is hurt and angry.
- Ari and Mara have become lovers in the default romantic route (or remain close friends if the player chose Choice 5-B).
- Jean-Paul and Varga's mutual attraction has intensified.
- Varga has collected emotional data and begun arranging encounters.

Lyra does not yet know the full extent of Steffan's motives or Varga's role.

She therefore does not break up with Steffan in this chapter. That occurs in Act IV before their semifinal rematch.

## D. Varga's causal role

Varga:

- Has access to relevant research schedules and some facility monitoring.
- Can manipulate meeting opportunities.
- Can obtain security footage through authorized or improperly acquired access.
- Facilitates the scandal leak through an intermediary.
- Collects emotional and synchronization data.

She cannot:

- Make Lyra desire Steffan.
- Control Dante's feelings.
- Force Ari and Mara to fall in love.
- Determine tournament results.

Her actions are unethical but do not erase other characters' responsibility for their decisions.

## E. Neural-system mystery

Ren experiences an advanced-assistance anomaly.

Angel discovers an unexplained alteration in the central diagnostic record.

Neither is evidence that Ren has already been incorporated into Nova's composite intelligence.

The anomaly may result from predictive assistance crossing the boundary between anticipating and initiating motor commands.

The distinction becomes more important when Ari makes the neural-program choice.

## F. Ganymede evidence

Ari's newly acquired authorization log establishes:

- Emergency cargo was prepared.
- Departure authorization was held.
- The internal timeline contradicts the public account.
- The hold passed through commercial operations.

It does not yet establish:

- The identity of every responsible official.
- The full political motivation.
- The causal contribution of each delay to individual deaths.
- The complete approval and cover-up chain.

Those discoveries remain for Act IV.

## G. Relationship state

Recommended state variables:

- `ARI_MARA_ROMANCE`: friendship / attraction / lovers / estranged / reconciled.
- `ARI_MARA_TRUST`: distinct from romance.
- `LYRA_DANTE_STATUS`: together / separated.
- `LYRA_STEFFAN_STATUS`: attraction / exploring relationship / separated.
- `VARGA_EXPERIMENT_EVIDENCE`: none / suspicious / partial / confirmed.
- `GANYMEDE_EVIDENCE_STAGE`: suspicion / verified discrepancies / decision chain / public disclosure.
- `NII_OFFER_STATUS`: pending / refused / limited / deep participation.

The chapter's romantic scenes can vary with earlier choices, but tournament progression must not depend on selecting flirtatious dialogue.

## H. Gameplay pacing

The main structural issue is that Ari has no official match between the previous chapter and the quarterfinals.

Suggested implementation:

- **Arcade Mode:** Skip most of Chapter 3 and move directly to the quarterfinal.
- **Cinematic Mode:** Offer the Mara sparring encounter as playable content, with the option to skip it.
- **Other pilot routes:** The group-finishing matches become playable when the selected pilot participates in them.

The game should not force additional tournament matches merely to manufacture action.

## I. Chapter 4 setup

Chapter 4 begins with the knockout bracket and Ari's decision concerning the Neural Integration Initiative.

### Default branch

Ari faces Mara.

The central emotional conflict involves their relationship, her need for prize money, and their disagreement about WAR's advanced neural program.

### Alternate branch

Ari faces Ren.

The immediate fight emphasizes the contrast between standard piloting and experimental neural enhancement.

Mara faces Crystal in the other quarterfinal.

Ari and Mara's relationship remains consequential even when they are not direct opponents.

### Shared mystery

Angel, Ren, and Crystal continue investigating the surviving Nova technology.

The chapter culminates in the revelation that **Kreissack's biological brain is still alive**.

---

**END — CHAPTER 3 v0.1**
